_source line 4841 · 2026-05-20T19:54:11.245654+00:00_

[System 触发审查]:
**Standard 20% SFT 中期值更新**

Agent 报告 @750 acc=60.5%（vs 你之前报告 @700 acc=61.14%）。趋势在下降。

如果终值 ~60.5%：
- GRPO (3-seed mean) = 59.84%
- Delta = 59.84 - 60.5 = -0.66pp → |Δ|<1pp 成立 ✅

如果终值 ~61%：
- Delta = -1.16pp → |Δ|<1pp 不成立 ⚠️

但注意：Paper Agent 之前 Table 2 用的是 Standard GRPO = 60.20%（seed42 单种子），不是 3-seed mean 59.84%。如果 SFT=61%，Δ = 60.20 - 61 = -0.80pp，仍在 |Δ|<1pp 内。

请确认：Table 2 的 Standard GRPO 列用的是 60.20%（seed42）还是 59.84%（3-seed mean）？这影响 delta 计算。

1	\section{Algorithm}
2	\label{sec:appendix_algo}
3	
4	\begin{algorithm}[h]
5	\caption{Controlled Pruning Comparison Framework}
6	\label{alg:rasp}
7	\begin{algorithmic}[1]
8	\Require Model $\mathcal{M}$, calibration data $\mathcal{D}_\text{cal}$, target sparsity $s$, scoring criterion $\mathcal{C} \in \{\text{Std}, \text{RASP}, \text{CKA}, \text{Rand}\}$, layer floor $\tau$
9	\Ensure Pruned and recovered model $\mathcal{M}'$
10	\Statex \textit{\% Stage 1: Shared Structural Analysis}
11	\For{each structure $h \in \mathcal{H}$}
12	    \State Compute importance $I(h)$ via Eq.~\eqref{eq:importance}
13	    \State Collect activations $\mathbf{X}_h$ on $\mathcal{D}_\text{cal}$
14	\EndFor
15	\For{each layer $\ell = 1, \ldots, L$}
16	    \For{each structure $h_i$ in layer $\ell$}
17	        \State $F(h_i) \gets \frac{1}{|\mathcal{H}_\ell|-1} \sum_{j \neq i} \text{CKA}(\mathbf{X}_{h_i}, \mathbf{X}_{h_j})$
18	    \EndFor
19	\EndFor
20	\Statex \textit{\% Stage 2: Criterion-Specific Scoring}
21	\For{each structure $h \in \mathcal{H}$}
22	    \State $S(h) \gets \mathcal{C}(I(h), F(h))$ \hfill $\triangleright$ Eq.~\ref{eq:standard}, \ref{eq:score}, \ref{eq:cka_only}, or random
23	\EndFor
24	\State Sort $\mathcal{H}$ by $S(h)$ ascending; prune lowest up to $s$, subject to $\tau$
25	\Statex \textit{\% Stage 3: Identical Two-Stage Recovery}
26	\State $\mathcal{M}_\text{warm} \gets \text{SFT}(\mathcal{M}_\text{pruned}, \mathcal{D}_\text{sft})$ \hfill $\triangleright$ restore EOS \& format
27	\State $\mathcal{M}' \gets \text{GRPO}(\mathcal{M}_\text{warm}, \mathcal{D}_\text{rlvr})$ \hfill $\triangleright$ recover reasoning
28	\State \Return $\mathcal{M}'$
29	\end{algorithmic}
30	\end{algorithm}

Perfect! I have found the answer. Let me verify once more by checking the exact content:

Based on my search of the recovery_aware_struct_prune project, I found **Table 2** (formally labeled as "tab2_decoupling" in `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_17/projects/recovery_aware_struct_prune/paper/tables/tab2_decoupling.tex`).

**GRPO Standard 20% accuracy value in Table 2:**

**59.84% ± 0.38pp** (the 3-seed mean)

**Exact quote from tab2_decoupling.tex (line 12):**
```
Standard 20\% & 59.29 & 59.84{\scriptsize$\pm$0.38} & $+$0.55 & 0.081 \\
```

This represents:
- **SFT Acc**: 59.29%
- **GRPO Acc**: **59.84% ± 0.38pp** ← This is the column value used in the table
- **Δ (pp)**: +0.55pp

The appendix confirms (section "Seed Reproducibility", line 92): *"Standard: mean 59.84% ± 0.38\,pp across the same 3 seeds."*

And the seed breakdown (line 122): *"Standard shows... non-overlapping ranges (CKA-only: 64.22--64.58%, Standard: 59.44--60.20%)."*

So the table uses **59.84%** (the mean across 3 seeds: 42, 123, 456), not the individual seed values of 59.44% or 60.20%.

[severity: info] Table 2 确认用 3-seed mean\n\n已核查 `paper/tables/tab2_decoupling.tex`：\n```\nStandard 20% & 59.29 & 59.84±0.38 & +0.55 & 0.081\n```\n- SFT = 59.29%（westd 版本）\n- GRPO = 59.84%（3-seed mean）\n- Δ = +0.55pp ✓ |Δ|<1pp 成立\n\n### 3. [severity: warning] Table 2 provenance 问题：混合两个不同 SFT 基线\n\nTable 2 的 3-seed mean (59.84%) 包含 seed42 (59.44%)，但：\n- **seed42 的 GRPO 是从 original `sft_standard_20` 训练的**（当前正在 eval 其准确率，轨迹 @1250=59.36%）\n- **seed123/456 的 GRPO 是从 `sft_standard_20_westd` (59.29%) 训练的**\n\nTable 2 的 SFT baseline 用的是 59.29%（westd），但 seed42 的实际 SFT 基线是 original 版本（终值待确认，目前轨迹在 ~59.0-59.4%）。这意味着 seed42 的 Δ 计算基线不匹配。\n\n正确做法二选一：\n- **方案 A**：Table 2 只用 seed123/456（同基线 westd 59.29%），2-seed mean = 60.05%，Δ = +0.76pp\n- **方案 B**：Table 2 用 3-seed mean 但在论文中注明 seed42 来自不同 SFT run（full disclosure）\n\n目前 Table 2 的 +0.55pp 含混合基线数据，审稿人如果要求复现细节可能会质疑。\n\n### 4.
